{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c6a7b65dd3a7710fc420e22615119f","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_9w5utukdy4g8_2","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/build_unified_taxonomy.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nUnified E-Commerce Product Category Taxonomy Builder\n\nThis script combines product categories from Amazon, Facebook, and Google Shopping\nto create a unified 5-level taxonomy.\n\nRequirements:\n- 10-20 broad categories at top level\n- 3-20 subcategories per parent at each level\n- Category names use \" | \" separator (max 5 words)\n- 70%+ representativeness of subcategories\n- < 30% word overlap between siblings\n- Balanced pyramid structure\n- Even distribution across sources\n\"\"\"\n\nimport csv\nimport re\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import defaultdict, Counter\nfrom sklearn.cluster import AgglomerativeClustering\nfrom sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer\nfrom sklearn.metrics.pairwise import cosine_similarity\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Constants\nINPUT_DIR = '/root/data/'\nOUTPUT_DIR = '/root/output/'\nMAX_LEVELS = 5\nSEPARATOR = ' > '\nUNIFIED_SEPARATOR = ' | '\n\ndef clean_category_text(text):\n    \"\"\"Standardize category text.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(text, str):\n        return \"\"\n\n    # Remove extra whitespace and convert to lowercase\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text.strip().lower())\n\n    # Remove special characters but keep spaces, hyphens, ampersands\n    text = re.sub(r'[^\\w\\s\\-\\&]', ' ', text)\n\n    # Normalize common variations\n    text = re.sub(r'\\b&\\b', 'and', text)\n    text = re.sub(r'\\b\\d+\\b', '', text)  # Remove standalone numbers\n\n    # Remove extra spaces again\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text.strip())\n\n    return text\n\ndef extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path, max_depth=5):\n    \"\"\"Extract hierarchical levels from category path.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(category_path, str):\n        return []\n\n    # Remove quotes and clean\n    category_path = category_path.strip('\"\\'')\n\n    # Split by separator\n    parts = [p.strip() for p in category_path.split(SEPARATOR)]\n\n    # Clean each part\n    cleaned_parts = [clean_category_text(part) for part in parts if part.strip()]\n\n    # Limit to max_depth\n    return cleaned_parts[:max_depth]\n\ndef load_and_process_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all three CSV files and process category paths.\"\"\"\n    datasets = {}\n\n    # Load Amazon\n    print(\"Loading Amazon data...\")\n    df_amazon = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}amazon_product_categories.csv')\n    amazon_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_amazon.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                amazon_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'amazon',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    # Load Facebook\n    print(\"Loading Facebook data...\")\n    df_fb = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}fb_product_categories.csv')\n    fb_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_fb.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                fb_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'facebook',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    # Load Google Shopping\n    print(\"Loading Google Shopping data...\")\n    df_google = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}google_shopping_product_categories.csv')\n    google_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_google.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                google_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'google',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    all_categories = amazon_categories + fb_categories + google_categories\n\n    print(f\"\\nData loaded:\")\n    print(f\"  Amazon: {len(amazon_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Facebook: {len(fb_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Google: {len(google_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Total: {len(all_categories)} categories\")\n\n    return all_categories\n\ndef create_name_variants(name):\n    \"\"\"Create different variants of a name for better matching.\"\"\"\n    variants = [name]\n\n    # Add plural/singular variants for some words\n    if name.endswith('s') and len(name) > 4:\n        variants.append(name[:-1])\n    elif not name.endswith('s'):\n        variants.append(name + 's')\n\n    # Remove common suffixes\n    for suffix in ['products', 'supplies', 'items', 'goods']:\n        if name.endswith(' ' + suffix):\n            variants.append(name.replace(' ' + suffix, ''))\n\n    return list(set(variants))\n\ndef jaccard_similarity(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity between two strings.\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not set1 and not set2:\n        return 1.0\n    if not set1 or not set2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    union = len(set1.union(set2))\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0\n\ndef word_overlap_ratio(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap ratio between two strings.\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not set1 or not set2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    min_len = min(len(set1), len(set2))\n\n    return intersection / min_len if min_len > 0 else 0\n\ndef cluster_categories(categories, target_count, level_name, min_similarity=0.3):\n    \"\"\"\n    Cluster categories using hierarchical clustering with TF-IDF similarity.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Create full path strings for better clustering\n    paths = [' > '.join(cat['levels']) for cat in categories]\n\n    # Use TF-IDF vectorization for similarity\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(\n        ngram_range=(1, 2),\n        max_features=5000,\n        stop_words='english'\n    )\n\n    try:\n        tfidf_matrix = vectorizer.fit_transform(paths)\n        similarity_matrix = cosine_similarity(tfidf_matrix)\n\n        # Use Agglomerative Clustering\n        # Convert similarity to distance (1 - similarity)\n        distance_matrix = 1 - similarity_matrix\n\n        # For small datasets, use direct clustering\n        if len(categories) <= target_count * 2:\n            # If we have fewer categories than target, don't cluster\n            return list(range(len(categories)))\n\n        n_clusters = min(target_count, len(categories))\n        clustering = AgglomerativeClustering(\n            n_clusters=n_clusters,\n            metric='precomputed',\n            linkage='average'\n        )\n\n        cluster_labels = clustering.fit_predict(distance_matrix)\n\n        # For each cluster, pick the most representative category\n        clustered = defaultdict(list)\n        for idx, label in enumerate(cluster_labels):\n            clustered[label].append(idx)\n\n        # Select representative categories from each cluster\n        representatives = []\n        for cluster_idx, member_indices in clustered.items():\n            # Score each member by its average similarity to others in cluster\n            best_score = -1\n            best_idx = member_indices[0]\n\n            for idx in member_indices:\n                # Calculate average similarity to other members in cluster\n                sim_sum = sum(similarity_matrix[idx][j] for j in member_indices if j != idx)\n                avg_sim = sim_sum / (len(member_indices) - 1) if len(member_indices) > 1 else 1.0\n\n                if avg_sim > best_score:\n                    best_score = avg_sim\n                    best_idx = idx\n\n            representatives.append(best_idx)\n\n        return sorted(representatives)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Warning: Clustering failed for {level_name}: {e}\")\n        # Fallback: return first target_count categories\n        return list(range(min(target_count, len(categories))))\n\ndef create_unified_name(categories):\n    \"\"\"Create a unified name for a cluster of categories.\"\"\"\n    if not categories:\n        return \"\"\n\n    # Collect all level names from these categories\n    all_names = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        for level in cat['levels']:\n            all_names.extend(create_name_variants(clean_category_text(level)))\n\n    # Count occurrences\n    name_counts = Counter(all_names)\n\n    # Get most common names (weighted by source diversity)\n    most_common = name_counts.most_common(10)\n\n    # Select top 1-3 words that best represent the cluster\n    selected_words = []\n    for word, count in most_common:\n        if len(selected_words) >= 3:\n            break\n\n        # Skip very short or very generic words\n        if len(word) < 3 or word in ['and', 'or', 'the', 'for', 'with']:\n            continue\n\n        # Check for overlap with already selected words\n        overlap = any(word_overlap_ratio(word, selected) > 0.5 for selected in selected_words)\n        if not overlap:\n            selected_words.append(word)\n\n    if not selected_words:\n        # Fallback: use the most common word\n        selected_words = [most_common[0][0]] if most_common else []\n\n    # Limit to 5 words\n    selected_words = selected_words[:5]\n\n    # Join with unified separator\n    return UNIFIED_SEPARATOR.join(selected_words)\n\ndef build_taxonomy_hierarchy(categories, current_depth, max_depth, parent_name=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Recursively build the unified taxonomy hierarchy.\n    Returns a list of dicts with unified category paths.\n    \"\"\"\n    if current_depth > max_depth or not categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Get categories at this level\n    level_categories = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        if len(cat['levels']) >= current_depth:\n            # Check if parent matches (if specified)\n            if parent_name:\n                parent_match = False\n                if len(cat['levels']) > 1:\n                    parent_in_cat = cat['levels'][current_depth - 2]\n                    # Check if parent matches (using similarity)\n                    if jaccard_similarity(parent_in_cat, parent_name) > 0.7:\n                        parent_match = True\n\n                if not parent_match:\n                    continue\n\n            level_categories.append(cat)\n\n    if not level_categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Group categories by their level name\n    level_groups = defaultdict(list)\n    for cat in level_categories:\n        level_name = cat['levels'][current_depth - 1]\n        level_groups[level_name].append(cat)\n\n    # Determine target count based on depth\n    if current_depth == 1:\n        target_count = 15  # 10-20 for top level\n    elif current_depth == 2:\n        target_count = 12  # 3-20 per parent\n    else:\n        target_count = 8   # Remove extra whitespace and convert to lowercase\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text.strip().lower())\n\n    # Remove special characters but keep spaces, hyphens, ampersands\n    text = re.sub(r'[^\\w\\s\\-\\&]', ' ', text)\n\n    # Normalize common variations\n    text = re.sub(r'\\b&\\b', 'and', text)\n    text = re.sub(r'\\b\\d+\\b', '', text)  # Remove standalone numbers\n\n    # Remove extra spaces again\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text.strip())\n\n    return text\n\ndef extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path, max_depth=5):\n    \"\"\"Extract hierarchical levels from category path.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(category_path, str):\n        return []\n\n    # Remove quotes and clean\n    category_path = category_path.strip('\"\\'')\n\n    # Split by separator\n    parts = [p.strip() for p in category_path.split(SEPARATOR)]\n\n    # Clean each part\n    cleaned_parts = [clean_category_text(part) for part in parts if part.strip()]\n\n    # Limit to max_depth\n    return cleaned_parts[:max_depth]\n\ndef load_and_process_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all three CSV files and process category paths.\"\"\"\n    datasets = {}\n\n    # Load Amazon\n    print(\"Loading Amazon data...\")\n    df_amazon = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}amazon_product_categories.csv')\n    amazon_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_amazon.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                amazon_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'amazon',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    # Load Google Shopping\n    print(\"Loading Google Shopping data...\")\n    df_google = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}google_shopping_product_categories.csv')\n    google_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_google.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                google_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'google',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    all_categories = amazon_categories + fb_categories + google_categories\n\n    print(f\"\\nData loaded:\")\n    print(f\"  Amazon: {len(amazon_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Facebook: {len(fb_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Google: {len(google_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Total: {len(all_categories)} categories\")\n\n    return all_categories\n\ndef create_name_variants(name):\n    \"\"\"Create different variants of a name for better matching.\"\"\"\n    variants = [name]\n\n    # Add plural/singular variants for some words\n    if name.endswith('s') and len(name) > 4:\n        variants.append(name[:-1])\n    elif not name.endswith('s'):\n        variants.append(name + 's')\n\n    # Remove common suffixes\n    for suffix in ['products', 'supplies', 'items', 'goods']:\n        if name.endswith(' ' + suffix):\n            variants.append(name.replace(' ' + suffix, ''))\n\n    return list(set(variants))\n\ndef jaccard_similarity(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity between two strings.\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not set1 and not set2:\n        return 1.0\n    if not set1 or not set2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    union = len(set1.union(set2))\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0\n\ndef word_overlap_ratio(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap ratio between two strings.\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not set1 or not set2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    min_len = min(len(set1), len(set2))\n\n    return intersection / min_len if min_len > 0 else 0\n\ndef cluster_categories(categories, target_count, level_name, min_similarity=0.3):\n    \"\"\"\n    Cluster categories using hierarchical clustering with TF-IDF similarity.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Create full path strings for better clustering\n    paths = [' > '.join(cat['levels']) for cat in categories]\n\n    # Use TF-IDF vectorization for similarity\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(\n        ngram_range=(1, 2),\n        max_features=5000,\n        stop_words='english'\n    )\n\n    try:\n        tfidf_matrix = vectorizer.fit_transform(paths)\n        similarity_matrix = cosine_similarity(tfidf_matrix)\n\n        # Use Agglomerative Clustering\n        # Convert similarity to distance (1 - similarity)\n        distance_matrix = 1 - similarity_matrix\n\n        # For small datasets, use direct clustering\n        if len(categories) <= target_count * 2:\n            # If we have fewer categories than target, don't cluster\n            return list(range(len(categories)))\n\n        n_clusters = min(target_count, len(categories))\n        clustering = AgglomerativeClustering(\n            n_clusters=n_clusters,\n            metric='precomputed',\n            linkage='average'\n        )\n\n        cluster_labels = clustering.fit_predict(distance_matrix)\n\n        # For each cluster, pick the most representative category\n        clustered = defaultdict(list)\n        for idx, label in enumerate(cluster_labels):\n            clustered[label].append(idx)\n\n        # Select representative categories from each cluster\n        representatives = []\n        for cluster_idx, member_indices in clustered.items():\n            # Score each member by its average similarity to others in cluster\n            best_score = -1\n            best_idx = member_indices[0]\n\n            for idx in member_indices:\n                # Calculate average similarity to other members in cluster\n                sim_sum = sum(similarity_matrix[idx][j] for j in member_indices if j != idx)\n                avg_sim = sim_sum / (len(member_indices) - 1) if len(member_indices) > 1 else 1.0\n\n                if avg_sim > best_score:\n                    best_score = avg_sim\n                    best_idx = idx\n\n            representatives.append(best_idx)\n\n        return sorted(representatives)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Warning: Clustering failed for {level_name}: {e}\")\n        # Fallback: return first target_count categories\n        return list(range(min(target_count, len(categories))))\n\ndef create_unified_name(categories):\n    \"\"\"Create a unified name for a cluster of categories.\"\"\"\n    if not categories:\n        return \"\"\n\n    # Collect all level names from these categories\n    all_names = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        for level in cat['levels']:\n            all_names.extend(create_name_variants(clean_category_text(level)))\n\n    # Count occurrences\n    name_counts = Counter(all_names)\n\n    # Get most common names (weighted by source diversity)\n    most_common = name_counts.most_common(10)\n\n    # Select top 1-3 words that best represent the cluster\n    selected_words = []\n    for word, count in most_common:\n        if len(selected_words) >= 3:\n            break\n\n        # Skip very short or very generic words\n        if len(word) < 3 or word in ['and', 'or', 'the', 'for', 'with']:\n            continue\n\n        # Check for overlap with already selected words\n        overlap = any(word_overlap_ratio(word, selected) > 0.5 for selected in selected_words)\n        if not overlap:\n            selected_words.append(word)\n\n    if not selected_words:\n        # Fallback: use the most common word\n        selected_words = [most_common[0][0]] if most_common else []\n\n    # Limit to 5 words\n    selected_words = selected_words[:5]\n\n    # Join with unified separator\n    return UNIFIED_SEPARATOR.join(selected_words)\n\ndef build_taxonomy_hierarchy(categories, current_depth, max_depth, parent_name=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Recursively build the unified taxonomy hierarchy.\n    Returns a list of dicts with unified category paths.\n    \"\"\"\n    if current_depth > max_depth or not categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Get categories at this level\n    level_categories = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        if len(cat['levels']) >= current_depth:\n            # Check if parent matches (if specified)\n            if parent_name:\n                parent_match = False\n                if len(cat['levels']) > 1:\n                    parent_in_cat = cat['levels'][current_depth - 2]\n                    # Check if parent matches (using similarity)\n                    if jaccard_similarity(parent_in_cat, parent_name) > 0.7:\n                        parent_match = True\n\n                if not parent_match:\n                    continue\n\n            level_categories.append(cat)\n\n    if not level_categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Group categories by their level name\n    level_groups = defaultdict(list)\n    for cat in level_categories:\n        level_name = cat['levels'][current_depth - 1]\n        level_groups[level_name].append(cat)\n\n    # Determine target count based on depth\n    if current_depth == 1:\n        target_count = 15  # 10-20 for top level\n    elif current_depth == 2:\n        target_count = 12  # 3-20 per parent\n    else:\n        target_count = 8   # 3-20 for deeper levels\n\n    # Cluster similar categories\n    cluster_indices = cluster_categories(list(level_groups.values()), target_count, f\"level_{current_depth}\")\n\n    unified_items = []\n\n    for cluster_idx in cluster_indices:\n        cluster_cats = list(level_groups.values())[cluster_idx]\n        unified_name = create_unified_name(cluster_cats)\n\n        if not unified_name:\n            continue\n\n        # Create unified path\n        unified_path = [unified_name]\n\n        # Recursively add subcategories\n        subcategories = build_taxonomy_hierarchy(\n            cluster_cats,\n            current_depth + 1,\n            max_depth,\n            parent_name=unified_name\n        )\n\n        # Build complete paths\n        if subcategories:\n            for subcat in subcategories:\n                full_path = [unified_name] + subcat['path']\n                unified_items.append({\n                    'path': full_path,\n                    'source_count': len(set(cat['source'] for cat in cluster_cats)),\n                    'categories': cluster_cats + subcat.get('categories', [])\n                })\n        else:\n            # Leaf node\n            unified_items.append({\n                'path': [unified_name],\n                'source_count': len(set(cat['source'] for cat in cluster_cats)),\n                'categories': cluster_cats\n            })\n\n    # Sort by number of sources (prefer categories from multiple sources)\n    unified_items.sort(key=lambda x: x['source_count'], reverse=True)\n\n    return unified_items\n\ndef validate_taxonomy(taxonomy):\n    \"\"\"Validate that the taxonomy meets all requirements.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Taxonomy Validation ===\")\n\n    # Count categories at each level\n    level_counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for item in taxonomy:\n        level_counts[len(item['path'])] += 1\n\n    print(\"\\nCategories per level:\")\n    for level in range(1, 6):\n        count = level_counts.get(level, 0)\n        status = \"✓\" if count > 0 else \"✗\"\n        print(f\"  Level {level}: {count} categories {status}\")\n\n    # Check level 1 count (should be 10-20)\n    level1_count = level_counts.get(1, 0)\n    if 10 <= level1_count <= 20:\n        print(f\"\\n✓ Level 1 has {level1_count} categories (within 10-20 range)\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\n✗ Level 1 has {level1_count} categories (should be 10-20)\")\n\n    # Check for source distribution\n    source_distribution = defaultdict(int)\n    for item in taxonomy:\n        for source in ['amazon', 'facebook', 'google']:\n            if any(cat['source'] == source for cat in item['categories']):\n                source_distribution[source] += 1\n\n    print(\"\\nSource distribution (number of unified categories containing each source):\")\n    for source, count in source_distribution.items():\n        print(f\"  {source}: {count} categories\")\n\n    # Check sibling overlap (sample a few)\n    print(\"\\nChecking sibling name overlaps (sample):\")\n    siblings_with_high_overlap = []\n    for item in taxonomy:\n        if len(item['path']) == 2:  # Level 2 siblings\n            siblings = [i for i in taxonomy if len(i['path']) == 2 and i['path'][0] == item['path'][0]]\n            if len(siblings) > 1:\n                for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n                    for sib2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n                        overlap = word_overlap_ratio(sib1['path'][1], sib2['path'][1])\n                        if overlap > 0.3:\n                            siblings_with_high_overlap.append((sib1['path'][1], sib2['path'][1], overlap))\n\n    if siblings_with_high_overlap:\n        print(f\"  Found {len(siblings_with_high_overlap)} sibling pairs with >30% overlap\")\n        for name1, name2, overlap in siblings_with_high_overlap[:3]:\n            print(f\"    {name1} vs {name2}: {overlap:.2%}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"  ✓ No excessive sibling overlaps found\")\n\n    return len(siblings_with_high_overlap) == 0\n\ndef generate_output_files(taxonomy, all_categories):\n    \"\"\"Generate the two required output CSV files.\"\"\"\n\n    # File 1: unified_taxonomy_full.csv\n    print(\"\\nGenerating unified_taxonomy_full.csv...\")\n    with open(f'{OUTPUT_DIR}unified_taxonomy_full.csv', 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        writer = csv.writer(f)\n        writer.writerow(['source', 'category_path', 'depth', 'unified_level_1', 'unified_level_2',\n                        'unified_level_3', 'unified_level_4', 'unified_level_5'])\n\n        for category in all_categories:\n            levels = category['levels']\n            depth = len(levels)\n\n            # Find matching unified taxonomy path\n            matched_path = None\n            for item in taxonomy:\n                # Check if this category maps to this unified path\n                category_full_path = ' > '.join(levels)\n\n                # Simple matching: if any category in the item matches\n                for cat in item['categories']:\n                    if ' > '.join(cat['levels']) == category_full_path:\n                        matched_path = item['path']\n                        break\n                if matched_path:\n                    break\n\n            if not matched_path:\n                # Try to find best match by checking if category is a subset\n                for item in taxonomy:\n                    for cat in item['categories']:\n                        cat_path = ' > '.join(cat['levels'])\n                        if category_full_path.startswith(cat_path) or cat_path.startswith(category_full_path):\n                            matched_path = item['path']\n                            break\n                    if matched_path:\n                        break\n\n            # If still no match, use the best fitting path\n            if not matched_path and taxonomy:\n                # Try to match by first level\n                if levels:\n                    first_level = levels[0]\n                    for item in taxonomy:\n                        if len(item['path']) > 0 and jaccard_similarity(item['path'][0], first_level) > 0.5:\n                            matched_path = item['path']\n                            break\n\n            if not matched_path:\n                matched_path = taxonomy[0]['path'] if taxonomy else []\n\n            # Pad path to 5 levels\n            path_padded = matched_path + [''] * (5 - len(matched_path))\n\n            writer.writerow([\n                category['source'],\n                category['original_path'],\n                depth,\n                path_padded[0],\n                path_padded[1],\n                path_padded[2],\n                path_padded[3],\n                path_padded[4]\n            ])\n\n    # File 2: unified_taxonomy_hierarchy.csv\n    print(\"Generating unified_taxonomy_hierarchy.csv...\")\n    with open(f'{OUTPUT_DIR}unified_taxonomy_hierarchy.csv', 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        writer = csv.writer(f)\n        writer.writerow(['unified_level_1', 'unified_level_2', 'unified_level_3',\n                        'unified_level_4', 'unified_level_5'])\n\n        for item in taxonomy:\n            path = item['path']\n            # Pad to 5 levels\n            path_padded = path + [''] * (5 - len(path))\n            writer.writerow(path_padded)\n\n    print(f\"\\n✓ Output files generated in {OUTPUT_DIR}\")\n    print(f\"  - unified_taxonomy_full.csv\")\n    print(f\"  - unified_taxonomy_hierarchy.csv\")\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Unified E-Commerce Product Category Taxonomy Builder\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # Remove extra whitespace and convert to lowercase\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text.strip().lower())\n\n    # Remove special characters but keep spaces, hyphens, ampersands\n    text = re.sub(r'[^\\w\\s\\-\\&]', ' ', text)\n\n    # Normalize common variations\n    text = re.sub(r'\\b&\\b', 'and', text)\n    text = re.sub(r'\\b\\d+\\b', '', text)  # Remove standalone numbers\n\n    # Remove extra spaces again\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text.strip())\n\n    return text\n\ndef extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path, max_depth=5):\n    \"\"\"Extract hierarchical levels from category path.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(category_path, str):\n        return []\n\n    # Remove quotes and clean\n    category_path = category_path.strip('\"\\'')\n\n    # Split by separator\n    parts = [p.strip() for p in category_path.split(SEPARATOR)]\n\n    # Clean each part\n    cleaned_parts = [clean_category_text(part) for part in parts if part.strip()]\n\n    # Limit to max_depth\n    return cleaned_parts[:max_depth]\n\ndef load_and_process_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all three CSV files and process category paths.\"\"\"\n    datasets = {}\n\n    # Load Amazon\n    print(\"Loading Amazon data...\")\n    df_amazon = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}amazon_product_categories.csv')\n    amazon_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_amazon.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                amazon_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'amazon',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    # Load Facebook\n    print(\"Loading Facebook data...\")\n    df_fb = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}fb_product_categories.csv')\n    fb_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_fb.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                fb_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'facebook',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    # Load Google Shopping\n    print(\"Loading Google Shopping data...\")\n    df_google = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}google_shopping_product_categories.csv')\n    google_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_google.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                google_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'google',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    all_categories = amazon_categories + fb_categories + google_categories\n\n    print(f\"\\nData loaded:\")\n    print(f\"  Amazon: {len(amazon_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Facebook: {len(fb_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Google: {len(google_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Total: {len(all_categories)} categories\")\n\n    return all_categories\n\ndef create_name_variants(name):\n    \"\"\"Create different variants of a name for better matching.\"\"\"\n    variants = [name]\n\n    # Add plural/singular variants for some words\n    if name.endswith('s') and len(name) > 4:\n        variants.append(name[:-1])\n    elif not name.endswith('s'):\n        variants.append(name + 's')\n\n    # Remove common suffixes\n    for suffix in ['products', 'supplies', 'items', 'goods']:\n        if name.endswith(' ' + suffix):\n            variants.append(name.replace(' ' + suffix, ''))\n\n    return list(set(variants))\n\ndef jaccard_similarity(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity between two strings.\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not set1 and not set2:\n        return 1.0\n    if not set1 or not set2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    union = len(set1.union(set2))\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0\n\ndef word_overlap_ratio(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap ratio between two strings.\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not set1 or not set2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    min_len = min(len(set1), len(set2))\n\n    return intersection / min_len if min_len > 0 else 0\n\ndef cluster_categories(categories, target_count, level_name, min_similarity=0.3):\n    \"\"\"\n    Cluster categories using hierarchical clustering with TF-IDF similarity.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Create full path strings for better clustering\n    paths = [' > '.join(cat['levels']) for cat in categories]\n\n    # Use TF-IDF vectorization for similarity\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(\n        ngram_range=(1, 2),\n        max_features=5000,\n        stop_words='english'\n    )\n\n    try:\n        tfidf_matrix = vectorizer.fit_transform(paths)\n        similarity_matrix = cosine_similarity(tfidf_matrix)\n\n        # Use Agglomerative Clustering\n        # Convert similarity to distance (1 - similarity)\n        distance_matrix = 1 - similarity_matrix\n\n        # For small datasets, use direct clustering\n        if len(categories) <= target_count * 2:\n            # If we have fewer categories than target, don't cluster\n            return list(range(len(categories)))\n\n        n_clusters = min(target_count, len(categories))\n        clustering = AgglomerativeClustering(\n            n_clusters=n_clusters,\n            metric='precomputed',\n            linkage='average'\n        )\n\n        cluster_labels = clustering.fit_predict(distance_matrix)\n\n        # For each cluster, pick the most representative category\n        clustered = defaultdict(list)\n        for idx, label in enumerate(cluster_labels):\n            clustered[label].append(idx)\n\n        # Select representative categories from each cluster\n        representatives = []\n        for cluster_idx, member_indices in clustered.items():\n            # Score each member by its average similarity to others in cluster\n            best_score = -1\n            best_idx = member_indices[0]\n\n            for idx in member_indices:\n                # Calculate average similarity to other members in cluster\n                sim_sum = sum(similarity_matrix[idx][j] for j in member_indices if j != idx)\n                avg_sim = sim_sum / (len(member_indices) - 1) if len(member_indices) > 1 else 1.0\n\n                if avg_sim > best_score:\n                    best_score = avg_sim\n                    best_idx = idx\n\n            representatives.append(best_idx)\n\n        return sorted(representatives)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Warning: Clustering failed for {level_name}: {e}\")\n        # Fallback: return first target_count categories\n        return list(range(min(target_count, len(categories))))\n\ndef create_unified_name(categories):\n    \"\"\"Create a unified name for a cluster of categories.\"\"\"\n    if not categories:\n        return \"\"\n\n    # Collect all level names from these categories\n    all_names = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        for level in cat['levels']:\n            all_names.extend(create_name_variants(clean_category_text(level)))\n\n    # Count occurrences\n    name_counts = Counter(all_names)\n\n    # Get most common names (weighted by source diversity)\n    most_common = name_counts.most_common(10)\n\n    # Select top 1-3 words that best represent the cluster\n    selected_words = []\n    for word, count in most_common:\n        if len(selected_words) >= 3:\n            break\n\n        # Skip very short or very generic words\n        if len(word) < 3 or word in ['and', 'or', 'the', 'for', 'with']:\n            continue\n\n        # Check for overlap with already selected words\n        overlap = any(word_overlap_ratio(word, selected) > 0.5 for selected in selected_words)\n        if not overlap:\n            selected_words.append(word)\n\n    if not selected_words:\n        # Fallback: use the most common word\n        selected_words = [most_common[0][0]] if most_common else []\n\n    # Limit to 5 words\n    selected_words = selected_words[:5]\n\n    # Join with unified separator\n    return UNIFIED_SEPARATOR.join(selected_words)\n\ndef build_taxonomy_hierarchy(categories, current_depth, max_depth, parent_name=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Recursively build the unified taxonomy hierarchy.\n    Returns a list of dicts with unified category paths.\n    \"\"\"\n    if current_depth > max_depth or not categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Get categories at this level\n    level_categories = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        if len(cat['levels']) >= current_depth:\n            # Check if parent matches (if specified)\n            if parent_name:\n                parent_match = False\n                if len(cat['levels']) > 1:\n                    parent_in_cat = cat['levels'][current_depth - 2]\n                    # Check if parent matches (using similarity)\n                    if jaccard_similarity(parent_in_cat, parent_name) > 0.7:\n                        parent_match = True\n\n                if not parent_match:\n                    continue\n\n            level_categories.append(cat)\n\n    if not level_categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Group categories by their level name\n    level_groups = defaultdict(list)\n    for cat in level_categories:\n        level_name = cat['levels'][current_depth - 1]\n        level_groups[level_name].append(cat)\n\n    # Determine target count based on depth\n    if current_depth == 1:\n        target_count = 15  # 10-20 for top level\n    elif current_depth == 2:\n        target_count = 12  # 3-20 per parent\n    else:\n        target_count = 8   # 3-20 for deeper levels\n\n    # Cluster similar categories\n    cluster_indices = cluster_categories(list(level_groups.values()), target_count, f\"level_{current_depth}\")\n\n    unified_items = []\n\n    for cluster_idx in cluster_indices:\n        cluster_cats = list(level_groups.values())[cluster_idx]\n        unified_name = create_unified_name(cluster_cats)\n\n        if not unified_name:\n            continue\n\n        # Create unified path\n        unified_path = [unified_name]\n\n        # Recursively add subcategories\n        subcategories = build_taxonomy_hierarchy(\n            cluster_cats,\n            current_depth + 1,\n            max_depth,\n            parent_name=unified_name\n        )\n\n        # Build complete paths\n        if subcategories:\n            for subcat in subcategories:\n                full_path = [unified_name] + subcat['path']\n                unified_items.append({\n                    'path': full_path,\n                    'source_count': len(set(cat['source'] for cat in cluster_cats)),\n                    'categories': cluster_cats + subcat.get('categories', [])\n                })\n        else:\n            # Leaf node\n            unified_items.append({\n                'path': [unified_name],\n                'source_count': len(set(cat['source'] for cat in cluster_cats)),\n                'categories': cluster_cats\n            })\n\n    # Sort by number of sources (prefer categories from multiple sources)\n    unified_items.sort(key=lambda x: x['source_count'], reverse=True)\n\n    return unified_items\n\ndef validate_taxonomy(taxonomy):\n    \"\"\"Validate that the taxonomy meets all requirements.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Taxonomy Validation ===\")\n\n    # Count categories at each level\n    level_counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for item in taxonomy:\n        level_counts[len(item['path'])] += 1\n\n    print(\"\\nCategories per level:\")\n    for level in range(1, 6):\n        count = level_counts.get(level, 0)\n        status = \"✓\" if count > 0 else \"✗\"\n        print(f\"  Level {level}: {count} categories {status}\")\n\n    # Check level 1 count (should be 10-20)\n    level1_count = level_counts.get(1, 0)\n    if 10 <= level1_count <= 20:\n        print(f\"\\n✓ Level 1 has {level1_count} categories (within 10-20 range)\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\n✗ Level 1 has {level1_count} categories (should be 10-20)\")\n\n    # Check for source distribution\n    source_distribution = defaultdict(int)\n    for item in taxonomy:\n        for source in ['amazon', 'facebook', 'google']:\n            if any(cat['source'] == source for cat in item['categories']):\n                source_distribution[source] += 1\n\n    print(\"\\nSource distribution (number of unified categories containing each source):\")\n    for source, count in source_distribution.items():\n        print(f\"  {source}: {count} categories\")\n\n    # Check sibling overlap (sample a few)\n    print(\"\\nChecking sibling name overlaps (sample):\")\n    siblings_with_high_overlap = []\n    for item in taxonomy:\n        if len(item['path']) == 2:  # Level 2 siblings\n            siblings = [i for i in taxonomy if len(i['path']) == 2 and i['path'][0] == item['path'][0]]\n            if len(siblings) > 1:\n                for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n                    for sib2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n                        overlap = word_overlap_ratio(sib1['path'][1], sib2['path'][1])\n                        if overlap > 0.3:\n                            siblings_with_high_overlap.append((sib1['path'][1], sib2['path'][1], overlap))\n\n    if siblings_with_high_overlap:\n        print(f\"  Found {len(siblings_with_high_overlap)} sibling pairs with >30% overlap\")\n        for name1, name2, overlap in siblings_with_high_overlap[:3]:\n            print(f\"    {name1} vs {name2}: {overlap:.2%}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"  ✓ No excessive sibling overlaps found\")\n\n    return len(siblings_with_high_overlap) == 0\n\ndef generate_output_files(taxonomy, all_categories):\n    \"\"\"Generate the two required output CSV files.\"\"\"\n\n    # File 1: unified_taxonomy_full.csv\n    print(\"\\nGenerating unified_taxonomy_full.csv...\")\n    with open(f'{OUTPUT_DIR}unified_taxonomy_full.csv', 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        writer = csv.writer(f)\n        writer.writerow(['source', 'category_path', 'depth', 'unified_level_1', 'unified_level_2',\n                        'unified_level_3', 'unified_level_4', 'unified_level_5'])\n\n        for category in all_categories:\n            levels = category['levels']\n            depth = len(levels)\n\n            # Find matching unified taxonomy path\n            matched_path = None\n            for item in taxonomy:\n                # Check if this category maps to this unified path\n                category_full_path = ' > '.join(levels)\n\n                # Simple matching: if any category in the item matches\n                for cat in item['categories']:\n                    if ' > '.join(cat['levels']) == category_full_path:\n                        matched_path = item['path']\n                        break\n                if matched_path:\n                    break\n\n            if not matched_path:\n                # Try to find best match by checking if category is a subset\n                for item in taxonomy:\n                    for cat in item['categories']:\n                        cat_path = ' > '.join(cat['levels'])\n                        if category_full_path.startswith(cat_path) or cat_path.startswith(category_full_path):\n                            matched_path = item['path']\n                            break\n                    if matched_path:\n                        break\n\n            # If still no match, use the best fitting path\n            if not matched_path and taxonomy:\n                # Try to match by first level\n                if levels:\n                    first_level = levels[0]\n                    for item in taxonomy:\n                        if len(item['path']) > 0 and jaccard_similarity(item['path'][0], first_level) > 0.5:\n                            matched_path = item['path']\n                            break\n\n            if not matched_path:\n                matched_path = taxonomy[0]['path'] if taxonomy else []\n\n            # Pad path to 5 levels\n            path_padded = matched_path + [''] * (5 - len(matched_path))\n\n            writer.writerow([\n                category['source'],\n                category['original_path'],\n                depth,\n                path_padded[0],\n                path_padded[1],\n                path_padded[2],\n                path_padded[3],\n                path_padded[4]\n            ])\n\n    # File 2: unified_taxonomy_hierarchy.csv\n    print(\"Generating unified_taxonomy_hierarchy.csv...\")\n    with open(f'{OUTPUT_DIR}unified_taxonomy_hierarchy.csv', 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        writer = csv.writer(f)\n        writer.writerow(['unified_level_1', 'unified_level_2', 'unified_level_3',\n                        'unified_level_4', 'unified_level_5'])\n\n        for item in taxonomy:\n            path = item['path']\n            # Pad to 5 levels\n            path_padded = path + [''] * (5 - len(path))\n            writer.writerow(path_padded)\n\n    print(f\"\\n✓ Output files generated in {OUTPUT_DIR}\")\n    print(f\"  - unified_taxonomy_full.csv\")\n    print(f\"  - unified_taxonomy_hierarchy.csv\")\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Unified E-Commerce Product Category Taxonomy Builder\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # Remove extra whitespace and convert to lowercase\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text.strip().lower())\n\n    # Remove special characters but keep spaces, hyphens, ampersands\n    text = re.sub(r'[^\\w\\s\\-\\&]', ' ', text)\n\n    # Normalize common variations\n    text = re.sub(r'\\b&\\b', 'and', text)\n    text = re.sub(r'\\b\\d+\\b', '', text)  # Remove standalone numbers\n\n    # Remove extra spaces again\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text.strip())\n\n    return text\n\ndef extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path, max_depth=5):\n    \"\"\"Extract hierarchical levels from category path.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(category_path, str):\n        return []\n\n    # Remove quotes and clean\n    category_path = category_path.strip('\"\\'')\n\n    # Split by separator\n    parts = [p.strip() for p in category_path.split(SEPARATOR)]\n\n    # Clean each part\n    cleaned_parts = [clean_category_text(part) for part in parts if part.strip()]\n\n    # Limit to max_depth\n    return cleaned_parts[:max_depth]\n\ndef load_and_process_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all three CSV files and process category paths.\"\"\"\n    datasets = {}\n\n    # Load Amazon\n    print(\"Loading Amazon data...\")\n    df_amazon = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}amazon_product_categories.csv')\n    amazon_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_amazon.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                amazon_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'amazon',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    # Load Facebook\n    print(\"Loading Facebook data...\")\n    df_fb = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}fb_product_categories.csv')\n    fb_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_fb.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                fb_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'facebook',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    # Load Google Shopping\n    print(\"Loading Google Shopping data...\")\n    df_google = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}google_shopping_product_categories.csv')\n    google_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_google.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                google_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'google',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    all_categories = amazon_categories + fb_categories + google_categories\n\n    print(f\"\\nData loaded:\")\n    print(f\"  Amazon: {len(amazon_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Facebook: {len(fb_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Google: {len(google_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Total: {len(all_categories)} categories\")\n\n    return all_categories\n\ndef create_name_variants(name):\n    \"\"\"Create different variants of a name for better matching.\"\"\"\n    variants = [name]\n\n    # Add plural/singular variants for some words\n    if name.endswith('s') and len(name) > 4:\n        variants.append(name[:-1])\n    elif not name.endswith('s'):\n        variants.append(name + 's')\n\n    # Remove common suffixes\n    for suffix in ['products', 'supplies', 'items', 'goods']:\n        if name.endswith(' ' + suffix):\n            variants.append(name.replace(' ' + suffix, ''))\n\n    return list(set(variants))\n\ndef jaccard_similarity(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity between two strings.\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not set1 and not set2:\n        return 1.0\n    if not set1 or not set2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    union = len(set1.union(set2))\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0\n\ndef word_overlap_ratio(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap ratio between two strings.\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not set1 or not set2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    min_len = min(len(set1), len(set2))\n\n    return intersection / min_len if min_len > 0 else 0\n\ndef cluster_categories(categories, target_count, level_name, min_similarity=0.3):\n    \"\"\"\n    Cluster categories using hierarchical clustering with TF-IDF similarity.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Create full path strings for better clustering\n    paths = [' > '.join(cat['levels']) for cat in categories]\n\n    # Use TF-IDF vectorization for similarity\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(\n        ngram_range=(1, 2),\n        max_features=5000,\n        stop_words='english'\n    )\n\n    try:\n        tfidf_matrix = vectorizer.fit_transform(paths)\n        similarity_matrix = cosine_similarity(tfidf_matrix)\n\n        # Use Agglomerative Clustering\n        # Convert similarity to distance (1 - similarity)\n        distance_matrix = 1 - similarity_matrix\n\n        # For small datasets, use direct clustering\n        if len(categories) <= target_count * 2:\n            # If we have fewer categories than target, don't cluster\n            return list(range(len(categories)))\n\n        n_clusters = min(target_count, len(categories))\n        clustering = AgglomerativeClustering(\n            n_clusters=n_clusters,\n            metric='precomputed',\n            linkage='average'\n        )\n\n        cluster_labels = clustering.fit_predict(distance_matrix)\n\n        # For each cluster, pick the most representative category\n        clustered = defaultdict(list)\n        for idx, label in enumerate(cluster_labels):\n            clustered[label].append(idx)\n\n        # Select representative categories from each cluster\n        representatives = []\n        for cluster_idx, member_indices in clustered.items():\n            # Score each member by its average similarity to others in cluster\n            best_score = -1\n            best_idx = member_indices[0]\n\n            for idx in member_indices:\n                # Calculate average similarity to other members in cluster\n                sim_sum = sum(similarity_matrix[idx][j] for j in member_indices if j != idx)\n                avg_sim = sim_sum / (len(member_indices) - 1) if len(member_indices) > 1 else 1.0\n\n                if avg_sim > best_score:\n                    best_score = avg_sim\n                    best_idx = idx\n\n            representatives.append(best_idx)\n\n        return sorted(representatives)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Warning: Clustering failed for {level_name}: {e}\")\n        # Fallback: return first target_count categories\n        return list(range(min(target_count, len(categories))))\n\ndef create_unified_name(categories):\n    \"\"\"Create a unified name for a cluster of categories.\"\"\"\n    if not categories:\n        return \"\"\n\n    # Collect all level names from these categories\n    all_names = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        for level in cat['levels']:\n            all_names.extend(create_name_variants(clean_category_text(level)))\n\n    # Count occurrences\n    name_counts = Counter(all_names)\n\n    # Get most common names (weighted by source diversity)\n    most_common = name_counts.most_common(10)\n\n    # Select top 1-3 words that best represent the cluster\n    selected_words = []\n    for word, count in most_common:\n        if len(selected_words) >= 3:\n            break\n\n        # Skip very short or very generic words\n        if len(word) < 3 or word in ['and', 'or', 'the', 'for', 'with']:\n            continue\n\n        # Check for overlap with already selected words\n        overlap = any(word_overlap_ratio(word, selected) > 0.5 for selected in selected_words)\n        if not overlap:\n            selected_words.append(word)\n\n    if not selected_words:\n        # Fallback: use the most common word\n        selected_words = [most_common[0][0]] if most_common else []\n\n    # Limit to 5 words\n    selected_words = selected_words[:5]\n\n    # Join with unified separator\n    return UNIFIED_SEPARATOR.join(selected_words)\n\ndef build_taxonomy_hierarchy(categories, current_depth, max_depth, parent_name=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Recursively build the unified taxonomy hierarchy.\n    Returns a list of dicts with unified category paths.\n    \"\"\"\n    if current_depth > max_depth or not categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Get categories at this level\n    level_categories = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        if len(cat['levels']) >= current_depth:\n            # Check if parent matches (if specified)\n            if parent_name:\n                parent_match = False\n                if len(cat['levels']) > 1:\n                    parent_in_cat = cat['levels'][current_depth - 2]\n                    # Check if parent matches (using similarity)\n                    if jaccard_similarity(parent_in_cat, parent_name) > 0.7:\n                        parent_match = True\n\n                if not parent_match:\n                    continue\n\n            level_categories.append(cat)\n\n    if not level_categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Group categories by their level name\n    level_groups = defaultdict(list)\n    for cat in level_categories:\n        level_name = cat['levels'][current_depth - 1]\n        level_groups[level_name].append(cat)\n\n    # Determine target count based on depth\n    if current_depth == 1:\n        target_count = 15  # 10-20 for top level\n    elif current_depth == 2:\n        target_count = 12  # 3-20 per parent\n    else:\n        target_count = 8   # 3-20 for deeper levels\n\n    # Cluster similar categories - we have groups of categories, need to cluster the groups\n    # First, flatten and create a representative from each group\n    group_representatives = []\n    for group_name, group_cats in level_groups.items():\n        # Use the group name as representative and include all categories in group\n        group_representatives.append({\n            'levels': [group_name],  # The level name\n            'all_cats': group_cats   # All categories in this group\n        })\n\n    # Cluster the group representatives\n    cluster_indices = cluster_categories(group_representatives, target_count, f\"level_{current_depth}\")\n\n    unified_items = []\n\n    for cluster_idx in cluster_indices:\n        # Get all categories from clustered groups\n        cluster_cats = []\n        for rep_idx in cluster_indices:\n            rep = group_representatives[rep_idx]\n            cluster_cats.extend(rep['all_cats'])\n\n        unified_name = create_unified_name(cluster_cats)\n\n        if not unified_name:\n            continue\n\n        # Create unified path\n        unified_path = [unified_name]\n\n        # Recursively add subcategories\n        subcategories = build_taxonomy_hierarchy(\n            cluster_cats,\n            current_depth + 1,\n            max_depth,\n            parent_name=unified_name\n        )\n\n        # Build complete paths\n        if subcategories:\n            for subcat in subcategories:\n                full_path = [unified_name] + subcat['path']\n                unified_items.append({\n                    'path': full_path,\n                    'source_count': len(set(cat['source'] for cat in cluster_cats)),\n                    'categories': cluster_cats + subcat.get('categories', [])\n                })\n        else:\n            # Leaf node\n            unified_items.append({\n                'path': [unified_name],\n                'source_count': len(set(cat['source'] for cat in cluster_cats)),\n                'categories': cluster_cats\n            })\n\n    # Sort by number of sources (prefer categories from multiple sources)\n    unified_items.sort(key=lambda x: x['source_count'], reverse=True)\n\n    return unified_items\n\ndef validate_taxonomy(taxonomy):\n    \"\"\"Validate that the taxonomy meets all requirements.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Taxonomy Validation ===\")\n\n    # Count categories at each level\n    level_counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for item in taxonomy:\n        level_counts[len(item['path'])] += 1\n\n    print(\"\\nCategories per level:\")\n    for level in range(1, 6):\n        count = level_counts.get(level, 0)\n        status = \"✓\" if count > 0 else \"✗\"\n        print(f\"  Level {level}: {count} categories {status}\")\n\n    # Check level 1 count (should be 10-20)\n    level1_count = level_counts.get(1, 0)\n    if 10 <= level1_count <= 20:\n        print(f\"\\n✓ Level 1 has {level1_count} categories (within 10-20 range)\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\n✗ Level 1 has {level1_count} categories (should be 10-20)\")\n\n    # Simple matching: if any category in the item matches\n                for cat in item['categories']:\n                    if ' > '.join(cat['levels']) == category_full_path:\n                        matched_path = item['path']\n                        break\n                if matched_path:\n                    break\n\n            if not matched_path:\n                # Try to find best match by checking if category is a subset\n                for item in taxonomy:\n                    for cat in item['categories']:\n                        cat_path = ' > '.join(cat['levels'])\n                        if category_full_path.startswith(cat_path) or cat_path.startswith(category_full_path):\n                            matched_path = item['path']\n                            break\n                    if matched_path:\n                        break\n\n            # If still no match, use the best fitting path\n            if not matched_path and taxonomy:\n                # Try to match by first level\n                if levels:\n                    first_level = levels[0]\n                    for item in taxonomy:\n                        if len(item['path']) > 0 and jaccard_similarity(item['path'][0], first_level) > 0.5:\n                            matched_path = item['path']\n                            break\n\n            if not matched_path:\n                matched_path = taxonomy[0]['path'] if taxonomy else []\n\n            # Pad to 5 levels\n            path_padded = path + [''] * (5 - len(path))\n            writer.writerow(path_padded)\n\n    print(f\"\\n✓ Output files generated in {OUTPUT_DIR}\")\n    print(f\"  - unified_taxonomy_full.csv\")\n    print(f\"  - unified_taxonomy_hierarchy.csv\")\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Unified E-Commerce Product Category Taxonomy Builder\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c6a848af02947062879f56417297b3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_xgdxqtfo8q25_2","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/build_unified_taxonomy_v2.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nUnified E-Commerce Product Category Taxonomy Builder - Version 2\n\nThis script combines product categories from Amazon, Facebook, and Google Shopping\nto create a unified 5-level taxonomy.\n\nRequirements:\n- 10-20 broad categories at top level\n- 3-20 subcategories per parent at each level\n- Category names use \" | \" separator (max 5 words)\n- 70%+ representativeness of subcategories\n- < 30% word overlap between siblings\n- Balanced pyramid structure\n- Even distribution across sources\n\"\"\"\n\nimport csv\nimport re\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import defaultdict, Counter\nfrom sklearn.cluster import AgglomerativeClustering\nfrom sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer\nfrom sklearn.metrics.pairwise import cosine_similarity\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Constants\nINPUT_DIR = '/root/data/'\nOUTPUT_DIR = '/root/output/'\nMAX_LEVELS = 5\nSEPARATOR = ' > '\nUNIFIED_SEPARATOR = ' | '\n\ndef normalize_category_name(name):\n    \"\"\"Normalize a single category name.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(name, str):\n        return \"\"\n\n    # Remove extra whitespace and convert to lowercase\n    name = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', name.strip().lower())\n\n    # Remove special characters but keep spaces, hyphens, ampersands\n    name = re.sub(r'[^\\w\\s\\-\\&]', ' ', name)\n\n    # Normalize common variations\n    name = re.sub(r'\\b&\\b', 'and', name)\n    name = re.sub(r'\\b\\d+\\b', '', name)  # Remove standalone numbers\n\n    # Remove extra spaces\n    name = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', name.strip())\n\n    return name\n\ndef create_unified_category_name(names):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create a unified category name from a list of similar category names.\n    Uses voting to select the most representative words.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not names:\n        return \"\"\n\n    # Count word frequencies across all names\n    word_counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for name in names:\n        words = normalize_category_name(name).split()\n        for word in words:\n            if len(word) >= 3 and word not in ['and', 'or', 'the', 'for', 'with', 'all', 'other', 'misc', 'general']:\n                word_counts[word] += 1\n\n    # Select top words\n    sorted_words = sorted(word_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    # Take top 3-5 most frequent words\n    selected_words = []\n    for word, count in sorted_words:\n        if len(selected_words) >= 5:\n            break\n\n        # Check if word is too similar to already selected words\n        too_similar = False\n        for selected in selected_words:\n            if jaccard_similarity(word, selected) > 0.7:\n                too_similar = True\n                break\n\n        if not too_similar:\n            selected_words.append(word)\n\n    if not selected_words and names:\n        # Fallback: use the shortest, most common name\n        best_name = min(names, key=lambda x: (len(x.split()), -names.count(x)))\n        selected_words = normalize_category_name(best_name).split()[:5]\n\n    # Limit to 5 words\n    selected_words = selected_words[:5]\n\n    # Join with unified separator\n    result = UNIFIED_SEPARATOR.join(selected_words)\n\n    return result\n\ndef jaccard_similarity(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity between two strings.\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not set1 and not set2:\n        return 1.0\n    if not set1 or not set2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    union = len(set1.union(set2))\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0\n\ndef word_overlap_ratio(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap ratio between two strings.\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not set1 or not set2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    min_len = min(len(set1), len(set2))\n\n    return intersection / min_len if min_len > 0 else 0\n\ndef extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path, max_depth=5):\n    \"\"\"Extract hierarchical levels from category path.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(category_path, str):\n        return []\n\n    # Remove quotes and clean\n    category_path = category_path.strip('\"\\'')\n\n    # Split by separator\n    parts = [p.strip() for p in category_path.split(SEPARATOR)]\n\n    # Clean each part\n    cleaned_parts = [normalize_category_name(part) for part in parts if part.strip()]\n\n    # Limit to max_depth\n    return cleaned_parts[:max_depth]\n\ndef load_and_process_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all three CSV files and process category paths.\"\"\"\n    datasets = {}\n\n    # Load Amazon\n    print(\"Loading Amazon data...\")\n    df_amazon = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}amazon_product_categories.csv')\n    amazon_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_amazon.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                amazon_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'amazon',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    # Load Google Shopping\n    print(\"Loading Google Shopping data...\")\n    df_google = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}google_shopping_product_categories.csv')\n    google_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_google.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                google_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'google',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    all_categories = amazon_categories + fb_categories + google_categories\n\n    print(f\"\\nData loaded:\")\n    print(f\"  Amazon: {len(amazon_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Facebook: {len(fb_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Google: {len(google_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Total: {len(all_categories)} categories\")\n\n    return all_categories\n\ndef cluster_level_names(level_names, target_count):\n    \"\"\"Cluster similar level names together.\"\"\"\n    if len(level_names) <= target_count:\n        return level_names\n\n    # Create TF-IDF vectors for similarity\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(\n        ngram_range=(1, 2),\n        max_features=1000,\n        stop_words='english'\n    )\n\n    try:\n        tfidf_matrix = vectorizer.fit_transform(level_names)\n        similarity_matrix = cosine_similarity(tfidf_matrix)\n\n        # Use Agglomerative Clustering\n        distance_matrix = 1 - similarity_matrix\n\n        n_clusters = min(target_count, len(level_names))\n        clustering = AgglomerativeClustering(\n            n_clusters=n_clusters,\n            metric='precomputed',\n            linkage='average'\n        )\n\n        cluster_labels = clustering.fit_predict(distance_matrix)\n\n        # Group by cluster\n        clusters = defaultdict(list)\n        for idx, label in enumerate(cluster_labels):\n            clusters[label].append(level_names[idx])\n\n        # Select one representative per cluster\n        representatives = []\n        for cluster_idx, names in clusters.items():\n            # Pick the most common/shorter name as representative\n            best_name = min(names, key=lambda x: (len(x.split()), -names.count(x)))\n            representatives.append(best_name)\n\n        return representatives\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Warning: Clustering failed: {e}\")\n        # Fallback: return first target_count\n        return level_names[:target_count]\n\ndef build_unified_taxonomy(categories, max_depth=5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build a unified taxonomy recursively.\n    Returns a nested dictionary structure.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nBuilding taxonomy at depth {6 - max_depth if max_depth <= 5 else 1}...\")\n\n    if max_depth == 0 or not categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Get categories at this level (by their level in the hierarchy)\n    level_index = 5 - max_depth  # 0 for top level, 1 for next, etc.\n\n    # Group categories by their level name at this depth\n    level_groups = defaultdict(list)\n    for cat in categories:\n        if len(cat['levels']) > level_index:\n            level_name = cat['levels'][level_index]\n            level_groups[level_name].append(cat)\n\n    # Determine target count based on depth\n    if level_index == 0:\n        target_count = 15  # 10-20 for top level\n    elif level_index == 1:\n        target_count = 12  # 3-20 per parent\n    else:\n        target_count = 8   # Build recursive structure\n        result = {\n            'name': unified_name,\n            'children': build_unified_taxonomy(group_cats, max_depth - 1),\n            'categories': group_cats,\n            'sources': set(cat['source'] for cat in group_cats)\n        }\n\n        unified_categories.append(result)\n\n    # Count categories at each level\n    def count_levels(tree, level=1):\n        counts = {level: len(tree)}\n        for item in tree:\n            if item['children']:\n                child_counts = count_levels(item['children'], level + 1)\n                for k, v in child_counts.items():\n                    counts[k] = counts.get(k, 0) + v\n        return counts\n\n    level_counts = count_levels(taxonomy_tree)\n\n    print(\"\\nCategories per level:\")\n    for level in range(1, 6):\n        count = level_counts.get(level, 0)\n        status = \"✓\" if count > 0 else \"✗\"\n        print(f\"  Level {level}: {count} categories {status}\")\n\n    #
