Remove extra whitespace and convert to lowercase\n    name = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', name.strip().lower())\n\n    # Remove special characters but keep spaces, hyphens, ampersands\n    name = re.sub(r'[^\\w\\s\\-\\&]', ' ', name)\n\n    # Normalize common variations\n    name = re.sub(r'\\b&\\b', 'and', name)\n    name = re.sub(r'\\b\\d+\\b', '', name)  # Remove standalone numbers\n\n    # Remove extra spaces\n    name = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', name.strip())\n\n    return name\n\ndef create_unified_category_name(names):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create a unified category name from a list of similar category names.\n    Uses voting to select the most representative words.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not names:\n        return \"\"\n\n    # Count word frequencies across all names\n    word_counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for name in names:\n        words = normalize_category_name(name).split()\n        for word in words:\n            if len(word) >= 3 and word not in ['and', 'or', 'the', 'for', 'with', 'all', 'other', 'misc', 'general']:\n                word_counts[word] += 1\n\n    # Select top words\n    sorted_words = sorted(word_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    # Take top 3-5 most frequent words\n    selected_words = []\n    for word, count in sorted_words:\n        if len(selected_words) >= 5:\n            break\n\n        # Check if word is too similar to already selected words\n        too_similar = False\n        for selected in selected_words:\n            if jaccard_similarity(word, selected) > 0.7:\n                too_similar = True\n                break\n\n        if not too_similar:\n            selected_words.append(word)\n\n    if not selected_words and names:\n        # Fallback: use the shortest, most common name\n        best_name = min(names, key=lambda x: (len(x.split()), -names.count(x)))\n        selected_words = normalize_category_name(best_name).split()[:5]\n\n    # Limit to 5 words\n    selected_words = selected_words[:5]\n\n    # Join with unified separator\n    result = UNIFIED_SEPARATOR.join(selected_words)\n\n    return result\n\ndef jaccard_similarity(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity between two strings.\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not set1 and not set2:\n        return 1.0\n    if not set1 or not set2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    union = len(set1.union(set2))\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0\n\ndef word_overlap_ratio(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap ratio between two strings.\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not set1 or not set2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    min_len = min(len(set1), len(set2))\n\n    return intersection / min_len if min_len > 0 else 0\n\ndef extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path, max_depth=5):\n    \"\"\"Extract hierarchical levels from category path.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(category_path, str):\n        return []\n\n    # Remove quotes and clean\n    category_path = category_path.strip('\"\\'')\n\n    # Split by separator\n    parts = [p.strip() for p in category_path.split(SEPARATOR)]\n\n    # Clean each part\n    cleaned_parts = [normalize_category_name(part) for part in parts if part.strip()]\n\n    # Limit to max_depth\n    return cleaned_parts[:max_depth]\n\ndef load_and_process_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all three CSV files and process category paths.\"\"\"\n    datasets = {}\n\n    # Load Amazon\n    print(\"Loading Amazon data...\")\n    df_amazon = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}amazon_product_categories.csv')\n    amazon_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_amazon.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                amazon_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'amazon',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    # Load Facebook\n    print(\"Loading Facebook data...\")\n    df_fb = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}fb_product_categories.csv')\n    fb_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_fb.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                fb_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'facebook',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    # Load Google Shopping\n    print(\"Loading Google Shopping data...\")\n    df_google = pd.read_csv(f'{INPUT_DIR}google_shopping_product_categories.csv')\n    google_categories = []\n    for _, row in df_google.iterrows():\n        category_path = row.get('category_path', '')\n        if category_path:\n            levels = extract_hierarchy_levels(category_path)\n            if levels:\n                google_categories.append({\n                    'source': 'google',\n                    'original_path': category_path,\n                    'levels': levels,\n                    'depth': len(levels)\n                })\n\n    all_categories = amazon_categories + fb_categories + google_categories\n\n    print(f\"\\nData loaded:\")\n    print(f\"  Amazon: {len(amazon_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Facebook: {len(fb_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Google: {len(google_categories)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Total: {len(all_categories)} categories\")\n\n    return all_categories\n\ndef cluster_level_names(level_names, target_count):\n    \"\"\"Cluster similar level names together.\"\"\"\n    if len(level_names) <= target_count:\n        return level_names\n\n    # Create TF-IDF vectors for similarity\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(\n        ngram_range=(1, 2),\n        max_features=1000,\n        stop_words='english'\n    )\n\n    try:\n        tfidf_matrix = vectorizer.fit_transform(level_names)\n        similarity_matrix = cosine_similarity(tfidf_matrix)\n\n        # Use Agglomerative Clustering\n        distance_matrix = 1 - similarity_matrix\n\n        n_clusters = min(target_count, len(level_names))\n        clustering = AgglomerativeClustering(\n            n_clusters=n_clusters,\n            metric='precomputed',\n            linkage='average'\n        )\n\n        cluster_labels = clustering.fit_predict(distance_matrix)\n\n        # Group by cluster\n        clusters = defaultdict(list)\n        for idx, label in enumerate(cluster_labels):\n            clusters[label].append(level_names[idx])\n\n        # Select one representative per cluster\n        representatives = []\n        for cluster_idx, names in clusters.items():\n            # Pick the most common/shorter name as representative\n            best_name = min(names, key=lambda x: (len(x.split()), -names.count(x)))\n            representatives.append(best_name)\n\n        return representatives\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Warning: Clustering failed: {e}\")\n        # Fallback: return first target_count\n        return level_names[:target_count]\n\ndef build_unified_taxonomy(categories, max_depth=5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build a unified taxonomy recursively.\n    Returns a nested dictionary structure.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nBuilding taxonomy at depth {6 - max_depth if max_depth <= 5 else 1}...\")\n\n    if max_depth == 0 or not categories:\n        return []\n\n    # Get categories at this level (by their level in the hierarchy)\n    level_index = 5 - max_depth  # 0 for top level, 1 for next, etc.\n\n    # Group categories by their level name at this depth\n    level_groups = defaultdict(list)\n    for cat in categories:\n        if len(cat['levels']) > level_index:\n            level_name = cat['levels'][level_index]\n            level_groups[level_name].append(cat)\n\n    # 3-20 for deeper levels\n\n    # Get unique level names and cluster if needed\n    unique_level_names = list(level_groups.keys())\n\n    if len(unique_level_names) > target_count:\n        clustered_names = cluster_level_names(unique_level_names, target_count)\n        # Filter groups to only include clustered names\n        level_groups = {name: level_groups[name] for name in clustered_names if name in level_groups}\n\n    # Create unified categories\n    unified_categories = []\n\n    for level_name, group_cats in level_groups.items():\n        # Create unified name for this group\n        names_to_unify = [level_name] + [cat['levels'][level_index] for cat in group_cats if len(cat['levels']) > level_index]\n        unified_name = create_unified_category_name(names_to_unify)\n\n        if not unified_name:\n            continue\n\n        # Build recursive structure\n        result = {\n            'name': unified_name,\n            'children': build_unified_taxonomy(group_cats, max_depth - 1),\n            'categories': group_cats,\n            'sources': set(cat['source'] for cat in group_cats)\n        }\n\n        unified_categories.append(result)\n\n    # Sort by number of sources (prefer categories from multiple sources)\n    unified_categories.sort(key=lambda x: len(x['sources']), reverse=True)\n\n    print(f\"  Created {len(unified_categories)} categories at level {level_index + 1}\")\n\n    return unified_categories\n\ndef flatten_taxonomy(taxonomy_tree, current_path=None, result=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Flatten the nested taxonomy tree into a list of paths.\n    \"\"\"\n    if result is None:\n        result = []\n\n    if current_path is None:\n        current_path = []\n\n    for item in taxonomy_tree:\n        new_path = current_path + [item['name']]\n\n        if item['children']:\n            flatten_taxonomy(item['children'], new_path, result)\n        else:\n            # Flatten taxonomy to get all paths\n    all_paths = flatten_taxonomy(taxonomy_tree)\n\n    # Create a mapping from original categories to unified paths\n    mapping = []\n\n    for category in all_categories:\n        # Find the best matching unified path\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0\n\n        # Try exact depth match first\n        category_depth = len(category['levels'])\n\n        for path in all_paths:\n            # Calculate similarity based on shared words\n            category_words = set(' '.join(category['levels']).lower().split())\n            path_words = set(' '.join(path).lower().split())\n\n            if category_words and path_words:\n                overlap = len(category_words.intersection(path_words))\n                score = overlap / len(category_words.union(path_words))\n\n                # Bonus for depth match\n                if len(path) == category_depth:\n                    score *= 1.2\n\n                if score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_match = path\n\n        # Count categories at each level\n    def count_levels(tree, level=1):\n        counts = {level: len(tree)}\n        for item in tree:\n            if item['children']:\n                child_counts = count_levels(item['children'], level + 1)\n                for k, v in child_counts.items():\n                    counts[k] = counts.get(k, 0) + v\n        return counts\n\n    level_counts = count_levels(taxonomy_tree)\n\n    print(\"\\nCategories per level:\")\n    for level in range(1, 6):\n        count = level_counts.get(level, 0)\n        status = \"✓\" if count > 0 else \"✗\"\n        print(f\"  Level {level}: {count} categories {status}\")\n\n    #
